公司簡介 
華夏成立於 1964 年 ， 屬於台聚集團成員之
一，為國內 PVC 第二大生產商，旗下轉投
資子公司 VCM 廠台氯（持股 87%） 。華夏
2025 營 收 結 構 包 括 PVC /VCM 產品  
66%、PVC 製品（膠布、建材、膠皮等）
24%、鹼氯產品 10%，外銷區域以亞洲區
域為主 ， 包括印度 、 孟加拉 、 東南亞 、 中東 、
中國等地區。華夏原料部分，VCM 由台氯
供應，EDC 主要由台塑及國外廠商供應，
乙烯則是由中油和國外廠商供應。

主要客戶： 
主要競爭對手：

葉家宏 bill.yeh@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

華夏 (1305 TT) 
PVC 供給壓力仍大，壓抑利差表現

永豐觀點 
原料乙烯高檔回落，中國 PVC 社會庫存壓力仍大，造成 PVC 市場行情反轉，利差
表現受限，故研究部維持保守看待華夏獲利表現 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議維持中立：中國 PVC 社會庫存壓力大，而反內卷政策仍未見顯著成效，
造成 PVC 供給壓力延續，華夏將進行產銷調節，降低庫存，優先以國內市場銷售
為主，並避開紅海市場競爭，尋求獲利機會，研究部考量原料乙烯報價仍高，將壓
抑 PVC 利差表現，故研究部預期華夏營運表現將持續承壓，預估 2026 EPS -1.27
元，維持中立投資建議。

ESG 評析 
華夏企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學)

專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

### Page 2

華夏為國內 PVC 第二大生產商：華夏成立於 1964 年，屬於台聚集團成員之一，
為國內 PVC 第二大生產商，旗下轉投資子公司 VCM 廠台氯（持股 87%） 。華夏
2025 營收結構包括 PVC /VCM 產品 66%、PVC 製品（膠布 、 建材 、 膠皮等）24%、
鹼氯產品 10%，外銷區域以亞洲區域為主，包括印度、孟加拉、東南亞、中東、中
國等地區。華夏原料部分，VCM 由台氯供應，EDC 主要由台塑及國外廠商供應，
乙烯則是由中油和國外廠商供應。 
1Q26 稅後 EPS 為-0.21 元，略低於預期：華夏 1Q26 營收 25.11 億元（+45.7% 
QoQ，+8.6% YoY） ，優於預期，主因美伊戰爭推升PVC 報價，且下游客戶擔心斷
料風險而積極購料備貨，帶動營收表現，毛利率 1.97%（+1.32 pts. QoQ，+6.26 
pts. YoY） ，低於預期，主因華夏低價原料庫存多用於4M26 生產，1Q26 所使用
之低價原料庫存比例低於預期，稅後虧損 1.2 億元（虧損縮小 QoQ，虧損縮小 
YoY），1Q26 稅後 EPS -0.21 元，略低於預期。 
2Q26 虧損幅度預期將收斂：展望 2Q26，中國製造業在五一長假後，開工復甦緩
慢，為去化過高的電石法 PVC 社會庫存，短期增加外銷低價傾銷亞洲市場，而因
美伊戰事趨於和緩，下游廠商購買力道也開始出現縮手，造成 PVC 報價於 5M26
開始出現行情反轉 。 研究部評估受新三輕設備事故影響 ， 華夏2Q26 稼動率將受影
響，因 PVC 報價預期較 1Q26 季增，將推升 2Q26 營收表現，且公司具備低價庫
存效應 ， 獲利表現亦有望得到改善 ， 基此 ， 研究部預估2Q26 營收 33.23 億元 （+24.3
％QoQ，+25.0％ YoY） ，毛利率8.82%（+6.85 pts. QoQ，+16.05 pts. YoY），
稅後虧損 0.2 億元（虧損縮小 QoQ，虧損縮小 YoY） ，稅後EPS -0.04 元。

PVC 供給壓力仍大，維持中立投資建議：展望後市，受到中國貨源低價傾銷亞洲
市場，及原料乙烯走勢疲軟影響，PVC 報價趨於下跌，而受制於原料乙烯報價位居
高檔影響，PVC 利差將面臨下行壓力，而中國反內卷政策仍未見顯著成效，造成
PVC 供給壓力延續，華夏將進行產銷調節，降低庫存，優先以國內市場銷售為主，
並避開紅海市場競爭，開發供需結構較為健康的海外市場，尋求獲利機會。研究部
考量目前中國 PVC 社會庫存水位仍位於近年同期新高水準 ， 中國電石法PVC 稼動

Data Centers: Asia Communacopia + Technology — Key Takeaways: AI
Infrastructure: Powering the Next Intelligence Cycle
19 May 2026 | 8:26PM HKT
Presenters: Mark Fong, CEO (Empyrion Digital), Yaniv Ghitis (Chief Commercial and
Investment Offic er, Digital Edge), Stephen Watts (CEO, PaleBlueDot AI)
Bottom Line: 1) Power as the key bottleneck affec ting construction timelines and

delivery capability, with public-private partnership on grid infrastructure builds
needed to bridge the gap. 2) AI compresses timelines, with single-tenant GPU
customers signing contracts in 6 weeks vs. 4–6 months for traditional hyperscalers.
3) Data center operators preserving fungibility to cater toward both traditional
and AI-firs t customers, e.g. hybrid air/liquid cooling, variable rack densities, and
delaying data hall fit-out to the last possible moment. 4) Supply would sell out

within 3–6 months at current demand levels if it existed, but expansion is
constrained by power, permitting, water, and increasingly community acceptance. 5)
Geopolitics raises compliance overhead but has not dampened underlying demand.
Japan, India, the Philippines flagg ed as key upside growth markets.
Key Takeaways
Power as the key bottleneck
While GPU shortages should normalize by 2028–2029; power is the keyn
constraint affecting construction timelines and delivery capability, because

resolving grid infrastructure issues requires 5 to 10 years.
Public-private partnership is the key to answer who bears responsibility forn
infrastructure builds: Governments should take the lead given power’s nature
as a national utility, with private operators and hyperscalers increasingly
investing directly in power generation and renewables to bridge the gap while
contributing through utility tariffs. Malaysia was highlighted as a market where

government-utility collaboration is working effectively.
Power efficiency (PUE) vs water tradeoff: Differ ent markets demand tailoredn
approaches based on local resource scarcity and pricing. Digital Edge highlighted

### Page 2

its Mumbai facility as a case study where it is sourcing treated wastewater from a
nearby purifica tion plant rather than potable water. The site features the largest
water-based chiller plant in its class and delivers PUE below 1.25.
Empyrion foresees that the four pillars of infrastructure builds (power, water, n
connectivity, permits) are being joined by a fifth: community acceptance.
Operators who successfully engage local communities will gain a competitive edge.

How shifting customer mix is affec ting data center operators
Single-tenant AI customers vs. traditional multi-tenant — differ ent lead times:n
Empyrion noted that single-tenant GPU customers show commitment on orders 15–
24 months before construction starts, locking in bespoke designs early. Traditional
multi-tenant customers, by contrast, expect only 3–6 months of lead time and more
standardized infrastructure. Empyrion aims to delay data hall fit-out to the last

possible moment to cater to both groups.
Hyperscalers vs. neoclouds — radically different commercial speed: New AI-firs tn
customers and neoclouds are pushing for modular and prefab designs to compress
delivery timelines. They also move faster commercially, with Empyrion noting new
contracts can close in 6 weeks vs. 4–6 months with hyperscalers. As a GPUaaS
provider who transforms energy into tokens, PaleBlueDot cited a Japan project that
delivered 6,000 GPUs in 89 days. Speed to fulfill the demand has become a key

differentiator from a customer perspective.
Operator response — modularity, fungibility: The panel agreed that hybridn
air/liquid cooling is now the default design choice to support both GPU-intensive AI
and traditional cloud workloads in the same facility. Digital Edge engineers its
facilities to support rack densities from 20kW to over 1MW — a wide envelope that
avoids over-optimizing for AI at the expense of other workload types.
Growth outlook

Biggest upside markets: India is expected to grow rapidly, supported by favorablen
demographics, deep engineering talent, and proximity to the Middle East. Digital
Edge is already executing on this thesis via its 300MW Navi Mumbai campus. Japan
was also flagg ed as a standout with government-backed initiatives driving aggressive
expansion; alongside the Philippines with reduced red tape and scalable power.
Training vs inference: PaleBlueDot noted that c.70% of its AI compute demand isn

now inference. Company expects the demand to continue to scale as
application-layer consumption has yet to be fully realized.
Boldest predictions and forward-looking themes: Empyrion framed the AI-drivenn
demand surge as non-zero-sum where traditional cloud workloads continue to
grow alongside new GPU and AI-first demand, expanding total market size rather
than redistributing it. On the supply side, consolidation among DC operators is
expected to improve efficiency and reduce power/chip constraints.

Geopolitics
Regulatory shifts have caused operational adjustments (e.g. enhanced duen

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y
ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

WinWay Technology Co Ltd  | Asia Pacific
No Changes in Fundamentals
With Continual Ramp Up in
Socket Capacity; OW
What's new? Winway shares fell 8.5% today vs. the Taiex up 2.2%, mainly on
concerns about market share loss in Vera CPU probe cards. Investors also worry
about the capacity ramp up given weak April sales (-19% M/M).
Our view: Our checks suggest no major changes in Winway’s Vera CPU market share
vs. MPI, with the ramp up already underway in 1Q. We still see no change to 2Q

revenue, which we expect to grow 15-20% Q/Q, though likely more June-loaded. We
view this pullback as a good entry point to accumulate a stock that enjoys longer
testing time and larger package size upgrades. The company will host its earnings
call next Tuesday, where we expect discussion of the future capacity plan and a CPO
revenue update.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
WinWay Technology Co Ltd (6515.TW)
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture
long-term value. We assume a 9.14% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium
5.5%), a payout ratio of 80%, a medium-term growth rate of 16.4%, and a terminal growth rate
of 4.5%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected adoption of hypersockets and MEMS probe cards

n Faster-than-expected SL T and burn-in socket introduction
n Stronger-than-expected edge AI demand
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected adoption of hypersockets and MEMS probe cards
n Slower-than-expected SL T and burn-in socket introduction
n No edge AI demand in the next few years

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

No turning back
Now at the centre of Google’s TPU strategy
Recent TPU roadmap changes suggest Google is no longer treating MediaTek as
merely an alternative supplier. Instead, it appears to be increasingly positioned at

the centre of Google’s next-generation TPU strategy, while Broadcom’s role
becomes more complementary. We believe the market is not yet fully reflecting
this strategic shift, and after raising our 2028 EPS forecast by 38%, we are now
68% above consensus. We lift our TP from NT$4,500 to NT$5,922 and reiterate
High Conviction Outperform.
Critical strategy changes in TPU v9 supply chains
Google recently cancelled Pumafish (Broadcom's original TPU v9) after the project

had kicked off only around a month, and replaced it with a new one, Whalefish.
Whalefish, is more like a version of ‘Sunfish Ultra’, which puts two Sunfish (TPU 8i)
together. This implies Broadcom's v9 is likely to stay at 3nm with HBM3e, while
MediaTek's v9 (Humufish, aka A5922) will migrate to 2nm with HBM4e.
No longer merely an alternative solution for Google
We believe Google’s latest TPU strategy marks a meaningful shift in positioning
between MediaTek and Broadcom. While MediaTek’s project was previously viewed

by the market as a secondary program with uncertain volume, we now see it
increasingly becoming the core platform within Google’s roadmap, with Broadcom’s
solution taking on a more backup or less strategic role. Reflecting this shift and our
supply chain checks, we raise our 2028 Humufish shipment forecast to 2.4m units
(from 1.6m) and increase our previously conservative ASP assumption to US$15k,
leading to US$36bn revenue contribution, or 63% of total sales in 2028SP.

Potential inflection points in smartphone
Our checks suggest MediaTek is working on supplying modified D9500 for
Samsung’s Galaxy S27 FE. However, the project is still being verified by Samsung
with fairly demanding requirements, and final market share will depend on the
results. We currently do not factor in any Samsung contribution but see potential
upside if the project enters mass production.
Earnings revisions and valuations
We raise our 2028 EPS forecast by 38% to reflect the higher TPU contribution,

bringing our TP to a Street high of NT$5,922 (up from NT$4,500), still based on 30x
2H27-1H28SP PE. Reiterate High Conviction Outperform.

### Page 5

No turning back MediaTek - HC O-PF
22 May 2026 5
Figure 7 Figure 8
Forward PE Forward PB
Source: SinoPac Source: SinoPac
Investment thesis
We are seeing greater visibility into MediaTek's TPU business, with Google asking it
to pull forward the schedule. Lukewarm smartphone demand and sustained margin
pressure are likely to remain key near-term drags, and we believe the market could
react negatively if China’s weekly sell-out data shows a disappointing trend at the

beginning of 2026, which is likely to create an entry point. However, we expect
investor focus to increasingly shift towards the company’s long-term opportunity
in AI semiconductors, and we view the current valuation as undemanding compared
with ASIC peers (trading at 30-55x).
Catalysts
1) Higher-than-expected GPM, 2) market share gain in the high-end smartphone
segment, 3) smoother verification process for TPUs.
Valuation details
We use PE as our valuation methodology as IC design fabless companies typically

deliver higher growth, and we believe the market will focus primarily on its earnings
outlook. We have previously used a historical PE range to value MediaTek based on
our evaluation of the company's product cycle at the time. Our valuation is about
+2sd of its five-year average to reflect robust EPS growth driven by better smart
edge business and higher TPU forecasts.
Investment risks
1) Worse-than-expected smartphone demand globally; 2) a greater-than-expected

impact from share loss in the smartphone SoC market; 3) higher wafer prices.

Document 072: 260526_daiwa_Makalot

Source PDF: all report pdf/260526_daiwa_Makalot.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-05-26
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: Makalot released its 3Q26 guidance today (26 May). The company has 
lowered its overall shipment expectation due to geopolitical uncertainties and structural 
shifts in pricing dynamics. While clients are conservative with longer-term orders, the 
company is keeping its production flexibility to adjust for short-term orders.

We have a Hold (3) rating on Makalot. For more information on the company, please refer 
to our latest memo, 1Q26 results slightly beat, on 4 May 2026.

What’s the impact  
• 3Q26 guidance. Makalot expects its 3Q26 shipment to grow by a mid-single digit % 
(4-5%) YoY and ASP to decline by 3%, with a gross margin target of 24%. For 2026, 
the company has lowered its guidance for shipment to grow by a low-single digit % 
YoY (down from +5% YoY guided earlier) with a flat ASP YoY (from -1% YoY) and 25% 
gross margin. The likely ASP drop in 3Q26 would be due to a client’s AI bidding 
system and more orders from distributors and online channels. The lower volume 
forecast for 2H26 is due to the Middle East war, higher oil prices and inflation, which 
make clients conservative about longer-term orders. Once the end demand recovers, 
clients are more likely to restock through short-term orders, which the company can 
quickly fulfil using its flexible capacity.

What we recommend 
We have a Hold (3) rating on Makalot. Based on our 2026/27E EPS, the stock is currently 
trading at PERs of 13.8x/12.6x, at the lower end of its past-3-year trading range of 11-26x. 
Key upside/downside risks: higher-/fewer-than-expected orders from key clients and 
inflation.

In the interests of timeliness, this document has not been edited.

ME MO  
 3Q26 guidance: slightly revised down

長榮航太(2645) 
買進, TP 230 (潛在報酬 50.82%)

Price(05/20)：152.5 
Target Price：230

長榮航太 26Q1 單季 EPS 為 2.58 元，毛利率為 27.54%，雙雙寫
下歷史新高，主要受惠於(1)維修量能提升與價格調整，以及(2)製
造廠稼動率優於去年同期，促營收及獲利表現均大幅優於去年同
期，合併營收 47.39 億，QoQ-1.91%，YoY13.12%。 
研究部對長榮航太展望持正向態度，係因： (1)全球客機與引擎需
求暢旺，同步帶動新機與MRO 商機；(2)服役機齡攀升，推升維修
需求與單機產值 ；(3)人力穩步擴編 ， 奠定長期製造與維修量能 ；(4)
積極切入新機型維修與零組件製造 ， 挹注長期成長動能。 研究部預
估 ， 在維修與製造雙引擎帶動下 ， 長榮航太全年營收為199.42 億，
YoY9.70%；在維修議價效益與製造量能提升下，預估毛利率為
25.89%，年增 5.55 個百分點；營業利益 44.58 億，YoY48.52%；
隨轉投資事業發展正向，預估稅後獲利 36.98 億，YoY80.83%；
EPS 為 9.87 元。 
評價方面 ， 公司過往5 年 PER 區間位於 16x-40x， 集中於20x-24x
區間考量到全球航空市場仍處上升週期 ， 公司維修業務與製造業務
持續穩定成長 ， 業外轉投資事業獲利改善 ， 研究部給予歷史本益比
上緣區間 23xPER，目標價 230 元，投資建議為買進。  
 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
長榮航太(2645)成立於 1997 年，為長榮集團旗下子公司，母
公司持有股權 58.44%，公司主要營運項目包含機體維修、發
動機零組件製造，目前為排名全球前十大 MRO 公司。客戶涵
蓋全球 40 家企業，涵蓋 UPS(美)、ATLAS(美)、ANA(日)、
長榮航空(台)。此外公司轉投資包含和奇異 (GE)合資成立的
「長異發動機維修」、與勢必銳(SPIRIT)成立的「長銳航材」
以及和艾姆勒、匯鑽成立的「長鑽科技」。

⚫ 價格調整效益顯現，26Q1 毛利率與 EPS 雙創歷史新高 
長榮航太 26Q1 單季 EPS 為 2.58 元，毛利率為 27.54%，雙
雙寫下歷史新高，主要受惠於 (1)維修量能提升與價格調整，
以及(2)製造廠稼動率優於去年同期，促營收及獲利表現均大
幅優於去年同期，合併營收  47.39 億，QoQ -1.91%，YoY 
13.12%；部門業務方面，(1)維修部門旗下各維修項目均呈現
年成長，貢獻 37.24 億，YoY 10.52%，QoQ 2.04%，(2)製造
部門貢獻 10.15 億，YoY 23.80%，QoQ -14.12%，主要由引
擎生產貢獻(佔比 83%，年成長 20%)

⚫ 除權息狀況 獲利表現方面，合併毛利率  27.54% ，季增 13.43 個百分
點，主要受 25Q4 獎金提列拉低基期，年增 3.69 個百分點，
主要受惠於 24H2 合約漲價以及整體維修量提升 ； 受惠營收與
毛利同步走升 ， 費用率下滑 ， 營業利益提升至 11.29 億，QoQ 
134.66%，YoY 35.55%；同時轉投資子公司長翼轉盈，稅後
獲利躍升至 9.66 億，QoQ 98.55%，YoY 73.11%。

⚫ 維修體量穩中向上，製造進入佳境，估 26 年 EPS 為 9.87 元 
研究部對長榮航太展望持正向態度，係因：(1) 全球客機與引
擎需求暢旺，同步帶動新機與  MRO 商機；(2) 服役機齡攀
升，推升維修需求與單機產值；(3) 人力穩步擴編，奠定長期
製造與維修量能；(4) 積極切入新機型維修與零組件製造，挹
注長期成長動能。

維修業務持續受惠長週期紅利，呈現價量齊揚： 
儘管全球航太供應鏈瓶頸逐步緩解 ， 如波音 737 MAX 有望於 
26H2 將每月生產上限提升至 47 架 ， 長期目標為 52 架，但
市場仍面臨部分品質問題與交付遞延。例如空中巴士  A320 
系列交付受限於 PW1100G 引擎品質問題，導致兩大飛機製
造廠的交機數依然低於目標值 。 供應鏈瓶頸使全球運力缺口高
達 5,000 架次，進而推升全球機隊平均機齡至全球機隊平均
機齡攀升至 15.1 年 ， 創歷史新高 。 其中客機機齡約為12.8 年，
貨機機齡為 19.6 年，帶動深度維修與大修需求擴張。 
為因應強勁需求，公司持續擴編人力，26Q1 維修及製造部門
合計已較去年同期增加約 100 名技術人員。同時，公司透過
增加班別與優化流程來提升維修總量 ， 並適時於合約到期時重
新議價，帶動整體營收與獲利成長。

受惠於供應鏈復甦，以及原廠 OEM 未交單持續成長，預期引
擎與機身組件製造業務呈現穩健增長 。 在新生產人員投入與終
端需求穩定成長帶動下 ， 預估 26Q2 至 26Q4 稼動率將持平
並小幅攀升。業外轉投資方面，子公司長異發動機已於 25Q4 
實現單季轉盈 ； 隨著 GE 對供應鏈釋出正向展望 ， 加上LEAP
引擎維修市場需求強勁，預期長異稼動率將有望穩步拉升，進
一步挹注整體獲利表現。

綜上所述，在維修與製造雙引擎帶動下，研究部預估長榮航太
全年營收 199.42 億，YoY 9.70%；在維修議價效益與製造量
能提升下，預估毛利率為 25.89%，年增 5.55 個百分點；營
業利益 44.58 億，YoY 48.52%；隨轉投資事業發展正向，預
估稅後獲利 36.98 億，YoY 80.83%；EPS 為 9.87 元。

⚫ 觀音二場與新品認證，成長期成長動能 
佈局中長期營運，長榮航太持續擴張「新機製造產能」與「新
製程認證」。(1)製造端，觀音二期廠房預計於 27Q1 交付空
巴首批新合約組裝產品 ， 並預期於27Q3 取得特殊製程認證後
投入零件自製 ； 波音新機型機翼組件也預計於27Q3 完成首件
交付。(2)維修端，配合母公司長榮航空預計於 2027 年引進
A350 客機，公司已提前取得 EASA A350 維修認證並建置妥
當維修量能。隨新產能陸續開出與新世代機型維修業務的銜
接，預期將為長榮航太帶來長期營收成長。

⚫ 結論與建議: 
長榮航太 26Q1 單季 EPS 為 2.58 元 ， 毛利率為27.54%， 雙雙寫下
歷史新高 ， 主要受惠於(1)維修量能提升與價格調整 ， 以及(2)製造廠
稼動率優於去年同期，促營收及獲利表現均大幅優於去年同期，合
併營收 47.39 億，QoQ -1.91%，YoY 13.12%。 
研究部對長榮航太展望持正向態度，係因： (1) 全球客機與引擎需
求暢旺，同步帶動新機與 MRO 商機；(2) 服役機齡攀升，推升維
修需求與單機產值 ；(3) 人力穩步擴編 ， 奠定長期製造與維修量能；
(4) 積極切入新機型維修與零組件製造，挹注長期成長動能。 
研究部預估，在維修與製造雙引擎帶動下，長榮航太全年營收為  
199.42 億，YoY 9.70%；在維修議價效益與製造量能提升下，預估
毛利率為 25.89%， 年增 5.55 個百分點 ； 營業利益 44.58 億，YoY 
48.52%；隨轉投資事業發展正向，預估稅後獲利 36.98 億，YoY 
80.83%；EPS 為 9.87 元。 
評價方面，公司過往 5 年 PER 區間位於 16x-40x，集中於 20x-24x
區間考量到全球航空市場仍處上升週期，公司維修業務與製造業務
持續穩定成長，業外轉投資事業獲利改善，研究部給予歷史本益比
上緣區間 23xPER，目標價 230 元，投資建議為買進。

3135.TT 凌航 
 產品報價迎補漲行情 2026 獲利強勁成長

[公司拜訪]   
   公司簡介 
凌航科技股份有限公司為動態隨機存取記憶體開發商。主要產品涵蓋 DRAM 模組、
Flash 快閃記憶體及 SSD 固態硬碟 。 公司具備強大的IC 測試技術與模組設計能力 ， 藉
由與上游原廠建立長期穩定的供應關係，為全球客戶提供一站式整合服務。  
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 260 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】無 
【大盤指數/股價】41368 / 195.5

➢ 1Q26 報價表現持續強勁，且產品組合逐漸優化 
1Q26 公司 DRAM 產品開始正式反映漲價後 ASP，同時出貨量方面，由
於適逢 2 月份春節假期，出貨天數減少，故預估位元出貨量將小幅季減
約 5%。1Q26 營收約 39.58 億元，QoQ+54.53%，YoY+177.96%，獲
利表現方面則受惠於報價漲勢強勁 ， 以及公司具相對低價庫存下 ，1Q26
毛利率提升至 30.35%，EPS 為 10.76 元，QoQ+149%，YoY+2,423%。 
➢ 2Q26 出貨開始反映報價強勁漲幅，獲利表現持續攀升 
考量到 1Q26 仍有部分客戶合約價尚未反映報價漲幅，凌航出貨反映報
價的時間應相對落後，故自 2Q26 全產品線才真正開始反應 DRAM 及
NAND 強勁漲幅 ， 預期2Q26 ASP 將維持強勁上行趨勢 ，DRAM 及 SSD 
ASP 預估分別季增逾 100% /150%，位元出貨量則大致與 1Q26 持平。
預估 2Q26 營收約 86.49 億元，QoQ+118.54%，YoY+397.37%，獲利
表現方面，受惠於 ASP 強勁漲幅以及產品組合優化，毛利率提升至
48.44%，稅後淨利為 31.2 億元，QoQ+264.53%，EPS 為 40.27 元。  
➢ 2026 年預計將迎來獲利噴發 
展望 2026 年，看好記憶體供需於短期不易緩解，市場將呈現賣方市場，
凌航客戶組成逐漸以工控 、 標案客戶為多數 ， 凌航得以轉嫁成本予客戶 ，
營收可望維持相對高檔，獲利則有望創新高。整體而言，預估凌航2026
年營收約 278.82 億元 ，YoY+261.91%， 毛利率為36.52%， 稅後淨利為
73.9 億元，YoY+1,557.4%，EPS 為 95.08 元。 
➢ 看好產業結構改變，公司朝工控發展，建議買進，目標價 260 元 
評價方面，參考過往淨值比區間約落在 1X~4X，預估 2026 年每股淨值
為 108 元，目前 PB 約 1.7X。看好公司客戶結構上的轉變，將有利於獲
利改善，故給予買進評等，目標價 260 元(2026F PB 2.4X)。投研部認為
目前記憶體需求仍處於快速上升階段，主要受惠於 AI 應用的大幅拓展，
因此產業需求出現結構性改變的情況下，將改變無論原廠或模組廠過往
景氣循環的獲利模式，預期隨著越多廠商簽訂 LTA，將逐漸改變市場對
於記憶體產業的評價模式，預期股價將迎來新一輪成長動能。

3135 凌航 加權指數

### Page 2

個股報告

公司簡介 
凌航科技股份有限公司創立於 1998 年，公司早期以 GSM 技術為基礎設計 SIM Card 
IC，並於 2007 年正式切入記憶體產業，轉型為動態隨機存取記憶體 DRAM 開發商。
目前資本額約為新台幣 7.75 億元 。 凌航科技定位為 「關鍵記憶體解決方案的製造商」 ，
主要產品涵蓋 DRAM 記憶體模組、Flash 快閃記憶體及 SSD 固態硬碟。公司具備強
大的 IC 測試技術與模組設計能力 ， 能針對不同終端應用提供客製化測試流程 ， 確保產
品在高效能與穩定性上達到國際品質標準 。 其營運模式高度聚焦於 B2B 與 B2G 市場 ，
藉由與上游原廠建立長期穩定的供應關係，為全球客戶提供一站式整合服務。  
凌航科技提供一站式記憶體整合服務 ， 其產品組合主要分為DRAM 記憶體模組 、Flash
快閃記憶體及 SSD 固態硬碟三大類。DRAM 產品：2025 年受惠於 DRAM 報價於
2Q25 開始上漲，故預估 DRAM 營收佔比將提高近八成，其中 DRAM 產品又涵蓋雲
端伺服器 RDIMM、標準型及領先同業推出的 LPCAMM2、CUDIMM 等新世代高階
產品。Flash 與 SSD：營收佔比約兩成，包含企業級 eSSD 與各種工業級存儲方案，
隨著市場對大容量存儲需求增加，營收貢獻度穩定提升。工控與利基型應用：公司積
極優化產品結構，將重心由消費型挪移至高附加價值的工控 IPC、邊緣 AI 及寬溫記憶
體方案  
圖 1：凌航產品營收組合

資料來源：公司, 國票投顧預估整理 
2025 受惠報價上漲，獲利顯著成長 
4Q25 記 憶 體 市 場 進 入 極 度 緊 缺 的 賣 方 格 局 ， 主 流 原 廠 如 美 光(MU.US) 、 三 星
(005930.KS)、SK 海力士(000660.KS)對通用型 DDR4/DDR5 的供貨採取限縮。凌航
營收組合主要為 DRAM 及 SSD，DRAM 自 3Q25 開始上漲 ， 而NAND Flash 自 4Q25
開始上漲，凌航 4Q25 出貨多為大幅漲價前的合約報價，故毛利率雖有在低價庫存的
幫助下提升，但並未能真實反映現貨報價漲幅。回顧 2025 年，隨著生成式 AI 帶動
HBM 與 DDR5 需求，產能排擠效應逐步發酵，公司策略性將庫存金額由 7 億元提升
至近 12 億元，以應對後續供給緊縮。進入2H25，記憶體合約價漲勢確立，公司憑藉
持有的低價庫存與漲價紅利，獲利動能顯著噴發。此外，工控產品營收佔比從 2024
年的 7.18%翻倍成長至超過 15%， 顯示出在市場缺貨時 ， 凌航具備快速切入利基市場
的競爭優勢。2025 年營收約 77 億元，YoY+40%，毛利率 10.04%，稅後淨利 4.46
億元，YoY+213%，EPS 為 6.33 元。

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
1Q26 報價表現持續強勁，且產品組合逐漸優化 
1Q26 公司 DRAM 產品開始正式反映漲價後 ASP，預估 1Q26 DRAM ASP 季增幅可
望逾 60%，同時由於 NAND 報價自 4Q25 開始調漲，預估 1Q26 SSD ASP 季增幅逾
4 成。出貨量方面，由於目前記憶體供需緊張，預期凌航將更加嚴格控貨，以維持健
康庫存水位，同時由於消費性產品對於價格波動較為敏感，導致電競產品佔比急遽縮
減，再加上 2 月份適逢春節假期 ， 出貨天數減少 ，故預估位元出貨量將小幅季減約 5%。
1Q26 營收約 39.58 億元，QoQ+54.53%，YoY+177.96%，獲利表現方面則受惠於
報 價 漲 勢 強 勁 ， 以 及 公 司 具 相 對 低 價 庫 存 下 ，1Q26 毛 利 率 提 升 至 30.35% ，
QoQ+11.24ppts ， YoY+25.31ppts ， 稅 後 淨 利 8.56 億元， QoQ+149% ，
YoY+2,423%，EPS 為 10.76 元。 
2Q26 出貨開始反映報價強勁漲幅，獲利表現持續攀升 
04/2026 營收約 38.53 億元 ，MoM+169.38%，YoY+607.83%， 營收大幅優於預期 ，
推測在出貨量方面，因受到物料交期拉長影響，加上 1Q26 工作天數較少，導致部分
訂單遞延至 4 月出貨，故 4 月出貨量有較顯著提升，預估 5 月、6 月出貨量將回復至
正常出貨水準 。 至於報價方面 ， 考量到1Q26 仍有部分客戶合約價尚未反映報價漲幅 ，
凌航出貨反映報價的時間應相對落後，故自 2Q26 全產品線才真正開始反應 DRAM
及 NAND 強勁漲幅，預期 2Q26 ASP 將維持強勁上行趨勢，DRAM 及 SSD ASP 預
估分別季增逾 100% /150%。此外，雖然前述表示 5 月、6 月出貨量將較 4 月下滑，
同時觀察對於價格波動較為敏感的消費/電競需求將轉趨疲弱 ， 然而看好工控 、 網通等
需求拉貨需求強勁，預期工控、網通營收占比將自 2Q26 開始顯著提升，進一步改善
獲利結構，預估 2Q26 位元出貨量大致與 1Q26 持平。整體而言，預估 2Q26 營收約
86.49 億元，QoQ+118.54%，YoY+397.37%，獲利表現方面，受惠於 ASP 強勁漲
幅以及產品組合優化，毛利率提升至 48.44%，QoQ+18.09ppts，YoY+44.84ppts，
稅後淨利為 31.2 億元，QoQ+264.53%，EPS 為 40.27 元。 
2026 年預計將迎來獲利噴發 
展望 2026 年，看好記憶體供需於短期不易緩解，市場將呈現賣方市場，模組廠由於
持有低價庫存，獲利將顯著成長，雖然凌航庫存水位相較於同業低，但考量到公司持
續積極確保貨源穩定，同時凌航客戶組成逐漸以工控、標案客戶為多數，客戶大多不
會因為價格上漲而降低購買數量，故凌航得以轉嫁成本予客戶，營收可望維持相對高
檔，獲利則有望創新高 。整 體 而 言 ， 預 估 凌 航2026 年營收約 278.82 億 元 ，
YoY+261.91% ， 毛 利 率 為 36.52% ， YoY+26.5ppts ，稅後淨利為 73.9 億 元 ，
YoY+1,557.4%，EPS 為 95.08 元。 
看好產業結構改變，且公司朝工控發展，建議買進，目標價 260 元 
整體而言 ，2025 年至 2026 年記憶體產業正處於結構轉變的階段 ， 受惠於三大原廠將
產能鎖定於 HBM 與伺服器 DDR5，導致傳統通路與工控市場出現嚴重的產能排擠，
使記憶體轉變為稀缺的戰略資源。凌航在過往記憶體市況處於下行階段之際，仍與原
廠維持良好關係，故看好凌航可望在貨源上保持相對穩定，同時透過嚴控產能，目前
確保庫存水位維持 3 個月左右。看好在目前市況下，公司會將業務重心從消費級挪移
至高附加價值的 AI 工控與邊緣運算領域 。 隨著產品驗證週期大幅縮短 ， 工控營收佔比
預計將顯著成長，進一步改善獲利結構。

評價方面，參考過往淨值比區間約落在 1X~4X，預估 2026 年每股淨值為 108 元，目

期公司將藉由這次的供需吃緊而與客戶簽訂 LTA，將有助中、長期營運獲利維持一定
水準。同時也看好公司客戶結構上的轉變，將有利於獲利結構的改善，故給予買進評
等，目標價 260 元(2026F PB 2.4X)。投研部認為目前記憶體需求仍處於快速上升階
段 ，主要受惠於AI 應用的大幅拓展，因此產業需求出現結構性改變的情況下，將改變
無論原廠或模組廠過往景氣循環的獲利模式，預期隨著越多廠商簽訂 LTA，將逐漸改
變市場對於記憶體產業的評價模式，預期股價將迎來新一輪成長動能。

元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (1 15)金管投顧新字第 009 號 
公司拜訪快報

聯合(4129) 
建議：維持買進 
結論：大幅擴編銷售團隊，深化美國市場佈局

一、2026 年英國、比利時、瑞士等市場將逐漸放量 
聯合歐洲 2025 年營收 19 億元，YOY +31%，成長來自
法國市場 ， 法國佔EMEA 比重約 70%， 聯合骨科在法國
市佔率約 10%，聯合透過產品、手術工具、教育訓練、
臨床支援、臨床紀錄資料庫、參加法國醫學會、法國期
刊發表等方式，成功打入法國市場，建立與醫師的信任
關係；展望 2026 年，聯合知名度已逐漸滲透至其他歐
洲市場，公司預期 2026 年英國、比利時、瑞士、西班
牙(經銷)與俄羅斯(經銷)等市場也逐漸放量。 
二、美國成立新總部，並大幅擴編銷售團隊 
2012 年聯合於美國成立子公司 ， 以直營方式自行開發醫
院通路，自 2026 年 2 月起，美國進口關稅為 10%，聯
合美國市場毛利率超過 80%，因此關稅衝擊不大。展望
2026 年，聯合將於美國成立新總部，並大幅擴編銷售團
隊，深化美國市場佈局；新品部分，2026 年預計取證的 
Momentum™ Acetabular Hip System（聯合優動髖臼系
統）採用模組化設計，可依據不同臨床需求靈活搭配，
提供更完整的人工髖關節解決方案，提升手術適配性與
臨床應用彈性；United Cone System（聯合重建錐形系
統）則聚焦於複雜骨缺損重建需求，可應用於多種骨缺
損情境，產品結合 3D 列印仿鬆質骨結構技術，有助於
提升骨整合效率與植入穩定性，進一步強化術後長期固
定效果。 
三、日本市場透過直營與經銷雙軌併行，成長動能強勁 
聯合骨科亞洲地區營收主要來自日本，日本佔營收約
10%，聯合於 2017 年於日本開設子公司，銷售聯合自
有品牌，且 2025 年與日本第四大關節廠商京瓷合作，
京瓷以貼牌方式銷售聯合的補骨柄產品 ， 合作產品 3Q25
出貨，且產品標示由聯合骨科製造，大幅提升聯合骨科
於日本市場的品牌知名度與能見度。 
四、2026 年擴編業務與研發團隊，預估 EPS 5.78 元 
預估聯合 2026 年營收 68 億元，YOY +20%，2026 年
成長來自美國與日本市場；毛利率部分，因為美國佔比
增加，且新台幣貶值，預估 2026 年毛利率上升 0.6%至
78.4%，然 2026 年費用明顯增加，預估 2026 年費用率
從 65%上升至 67%，因為業務與研發團隊擴編，並增加
參展、行銷預算，預估 2026 年 EPS 5.78 元。 
五、維持買進理由 
聯合在全球人工關節市佔率僅 0.6%成長空間大 ， 聯合於
歐、美、日市場已建置完整通路由聯合自營，1Q26 開
始大幅擴編銷售團隊，預期 3Q~4Q 將開始發酵，聯合
投資評等維持買進，目標價 116 元(20X2026EPS)。

瑞儀 (6176 TT) 
沉潛當中

永豐觀點 
短期營運處於逆風當中，新事業尚需時間發酵。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議中立 ：大客戶於其產品逐漸導入 OLED 面板，預期將明顯影響瑞儀
2026~2027 年的營運表現，新的營運動能須待 2028 年，考量短期展望保守，維
持中立的評等。

ESG 評析 
瑞儀企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

### Page 2

2

公司簡介：瑞儀為背光模組廠，自 2023 年起開啟一系列轉型，08/2023 併購芬蘭 
Nanocom 取得微米與奈米級的微結構光學薄膜壓印技術。 09/2024 年購丹麥 
NILT，取得奈米級微結構開發與晶圓級光學元件代工能力， 06/2025 收購芬蘭 
Inkron， 其專精於奈米壓印所需的 「高階光學鍍膜與化學合成材料」 。打造出從 「材
料、設計、模具到量產」 的一站式超穎光學製程平台，將技術實力從傳統背光跨向
奈米級光學製程；1Q26 營收比重：IT 佔 90%，Auto 佔 7.5%，FLG(Front Light 
Guide)佔 0.7%，Metalens 佔 0.2%。 
2026~2027 年營運逆風：IT 佔營收比重 90%，其中主要為平板與 NB 的背光模
組，大客戶為Apple，受到客戶產品逐漸轉向使用 OLED 面板、與晶片、記憶體短
缺議題，預估 2Q26 營收將呈現年衰退，目前 3Q26 的訂單能見度不高；iPad Pro
已於 2024 年導入 OLED 面板 ， 據了解iPad mini 與 iPad air 或將於 2H26 與 2027
年陸續導入 OLED 面板，另外 Macbook 系列或將由 Macbook Pro 優先導入
OLED 面板，時間點落於 2026 年底~2027 年；由於 OLED 面板無須背光模組，
這樣的趨勢將不利瑞儀未來 1 年半的營運表現。 
聚焦新產品，目標 2028 年營收比重達 20%~25%：瑞儀目前的新產品發展主要
聚焦於超引透鏡 (Metalens) 、波導  (Waveguide) 與光學管理模片 (Light 
Management Film,LMF) 三大領域，目標 2028 年這些產品貢獻營收比重 20%~ 
25%； 
(1) Metalens 已於 2025 年在檳城廠達成小規模量產，年出貨量超過百萬顆，並計
畫於 2026 年投入資本支出再增加兩條主力線 ，4Q26 完成產能爬坡 ， 瑞儀採取設
計、模具與材料的垂直整合策略，利用奈米壓印技術在玻璃基材上製作透鏡，達到
輕薄短小的光學處理效果，相較於半導體 DUV 製程，奈米壓印在量產成本與設計
疊代上更具優勢，應用可涵蓋智慧眼鏡、手機、平板、車載感測、衛星、無人機及
機器人視覺等領域。 
(2) Waveguide：鎖定 AR 智慧眼鏡市場，旨在解決傳統光學元件過於厚重及設計
彈性不足的問題， 針對目前產業良率普遍不佳且直接刻蝕成本極高的痛點， 利用奈
米壓印技術提供兼具高產能、高品質與成本競爭力的解決方案 ，目前專屬生產基地
高雄七廠預計於 02/2027 年進駐設備，並於 3Q27 開始營運，雖然工廠仍在興建
中 ， 但瑞儀的策略性零組件 （如模具與樹脂材料） 已先行供應給全球眼鏡大廠使用 ，
成功卡位科技巨頭的核心供應鏈；此外，波導技術亦具備多元應用價值，除消費性
電子外，已獲得軍用領域（如供應北約歐洲國家的軍規訂單） 與醫療、機器人等高
門檻市場的採用。 
(3) 光學管理模片(Light Management Film, LMF)， 該產品具備強大的光學效能提
升功能，包含抗污、抗炫與抗反光，能顯著改善顯示設備的視覺品質，應用範圍廣
泛，可從手機等小尺寸裝置一路延伸至中大尺寸的顯示設備。  
財務預估 ：預估 2026 年營收 457 億元(-6.3%YoY)， 毛利率22.3%， 稅後EPS 8.48
元。

光纖市場的供需緊張持續加劇， Nvidia 也意識到此一情境的緊
迫，因此先前宣布將入股 Corning 並合作擴增北美當地光纖及連
接器產能 。 沛亨即便在握有相對充足的原料庫存下仍僅能滿足客戶
不到一半需求的訂單 ， 為此公司也上調年底擴產目標 ， 由原先2025
年產量的 3 倍上修至 5 倍。綜合以上，研究部預估沛亨 2026 年
EPS 16.62 元，2027 年 EPS 27.82 元，目前 PE 為 17.6x，考量
光纖市場的供需緊張情境持續加劇 ， 另外沛亨在相對應的產能規劃
也不斷提高，因此上調 PER 至 25x，目標價 700 元，建議買進。  
⚫ 公司基本資料 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
沛亨半導體(6291)成立於 1992 年 ， 一開始本業為類比IC 設計廠，
2018 年加入振曜集團，目前產品分為網通產品（電源管理 IC、網
路產品）和電腦周邊（揚聲器、光纖）。其中光纖產品自 2022 年
收購超昱光電後才加入業務，產品主要交給資料中心用光纖 、跳
線，上游原材料來自於美、日系供應商。2025 年產品組合電腦週
邊 65%、網通產品 35%。

 ⚫ 近況更新 
光纖出貨續強，毛利率短期受工作天數影響 
沛亨 26Q1 營收 6.6 億元 ，Q+10.6%，Y+49.5%， 毛利率37.50%，
稅後淨利 1.3 億元，Q+3.5%，Y+186.9%，EPS 3.00 元。電腦及
周邊設備占比已來到 70%，顯現光纖產品的出貨動能強勁，稼動
率持續攀升，產品組合相對優化，然而面臨農曆新年工作天數較
少，為了能滿足客戶需求的訂單，公司加班趕工所產生較多的相關
成本使毛利率暫時性下滑，費用方面則控制得宜，因此本業獲利持
續攀升。

光纖稼動率持續攀升，營運表現有望續創新高 
沛亨 4 月營收 2.4 億元，M-3.5%，Y+42.1%。光纖延續去年底來
的強勁動能 ， 公司表示目前整體光纖市場供給緊張態勢不僅沒有緩
解甚至更加嚴峻，已觀察到光纖價格較去年成長了倍數之上，然而
在擁有充足的庫存和提前備貨的情況下 ， 仍會持續努力提升稼動率
來滿足客戶需求 。 綜合以上 ， 研究部預估沛亨26Q2 營收 7.9 億元 ，
Q+20.2%，Y+53.5%，毛利率 40.87%，稅後淨利 1.5 億元，
Q+18.0%，Y+976.3%，EPS 3.54 元。

全球光纖供給緊張，沛亨上調擴產目標至 5 倍 
已明顯感受到光纖供給的緊缺 ，Nvidia 於 5/6 公告預付 5 億美元認
股權取得 300 萬股 Corning 的股份 ，加計 180 美元 1500 萬股的追
加認購權，總計最高達 32 億美元的投資 。根據雙方合作協議，
Corning 將於北卡羅來納州與德州新建三座製造廠，提升在美國本
土連接器產能 10 倍以及 50%的光纖產能 。 由此可見在未來世代下
Nvidia 的平台也將逐步提高對於光通訊傳輸的依賴 ， 光纖的使用場
景將逐步從 Scale-Across 到 Scale-Out，甚至隨著 CPO 的導入在
Scale-UP 市場的滲透率快速攀升。相比於前次法說會，目前沛亨

在手訂單已談到 27Q1，然而公司仍僅能達成不足客戶一半的需
求 ， 因此決議再進一步提高今年底的擴產幅度目標 ， 將從原先2025
年產量的 3 倍提升至 5 倍 ， 此外先前公司也已公告將進行私募進行
增資，以滿足原料及擴產等相關支出，研究部持續正向看待光纖市
場在至少近一兩年內仍屬於賣方市場 ， 且在沛亨的競爭優勢及上下
游緊密的關係下，仍將能達成優於產業成長的營運表現。綜合以
上，研究部預估沛亨 2026 年營收 32.6 億元，Y+53.3%，毛利率
41.13%，稅後淨利 7.0 億元，Y+151.8%，EPS 16.62 元；2027
年營收 46.2 億元，Y+41.5%，毛利率 44.10%，稅後淨利 11.7 億
元，Y+67.4%，EPS 27.82 元。

結論 
光纖市場的供需緊張持續加劇 ，Nvidia 也意識到此一情境的緊迫，
因此先前宣布將入股 Corning 並合作擴增北美當地光纖及連接器
產能 。 沛亨即便在握有相對充足的原料庫存下仍僅能滿足客戶不到
一半需求的訂單，為此公司也上調年底擴產目標，由原先 2025 年
產量的 3 倍上修至 5 倍 。 綜合以上 ， 研究部預估沛亨2026 年 EPS 
16.62 元，2027 年 EPS 27.82 元，目前PE 為 17.6x，考量光纖市
場的供需緊張情境持續加劇 ， 另外沛亨在相對應的產能規劃也不斷
提高，因此上調 PER 至 25x，目標價 700 元，建議買進。

6957 裕慶-KY(Horizon)

報告日期： 2026/05/22 研 究 員：洪  國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 公告 1Q26 稅後 EPS 1.51 元 
在主要客戶 1Q26 積極出貨帶動，裕慶 KY 1Q26 營收金額 10.08
億元， YoY+28.64% ，QoQ-12.37% ，受到 1~2 月仍有高額關稅影
響，營業利益 1.01 億元， YoY+65.33% ，QoQ-68.78% ，稅後盈
餘 0.83 億元， YoY+29.03% ，QoQ-68.52% ，稅後 EPS 1.51 元。 
二. 預估 2Q26 稅後 EPS 3.48 元 
裕慶 KY 04/2026 營收金額 3.68 億元 ，YoY+53.31% ，MoM-26.6% 。 
在商用展示架客戶訂單持續回溫，預估裕慶 KY 2Q26 營收金額
11.34 億元， YoY+40.9% ，QoQ+12.5% ，預估營業利益 2.38 億
元， YoY+516.67% ，QoQ+135.4% ，預估稅後盈餘 1.93 億元，
YoY+414.86% ，QoQ+134.08% ，預估稅後 EPS 3.48 元。  
三 . 預估商用展示架 2026 年營收金額 43 億元，
YoY+24.64% 
商用展示架 2026 年營運成長動能： 1，美國家居裝修零售量販店
在自動灑水展示架出貨量增加，預估貢獻 2026 年營收金額 8~8.5
億元， YoY+42~51% 。2，美國折扣零售百貨持續增加原有門市汰
舊換新訂單，加上新店展示架訂單，預期貢獻 2026 年營收金額
6~6.5 億元， YoY+130~170% 。3，美國跨國零售公司，在零售賣
場的展示架翻新計畫，新據點展示架，加上客戶將商示架訂單集
中至裕慶 ， 預計2026 年貢獻營收金額 4.5~5 億元 ，YoY+42~59% 。
預估商用展示架 2026 年營收 43 億元， YoY+24.64% 。

四. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 13.00 元 
裕慶 KY 2026 年在商用展示架新客戶如美國家居裝修零售量販
店，美國折扣零售百貨與新客戶訂單挹注，帶動裕慶 KY 2026 年
獲利恢成長態勢 。 宏遠預估裕慶 KY 2026 年營收金額 46.00 億元 ，
YoY+20.3% ，預估營業利益 9.08 億元， YoY+28.76% ，預估稅後
盈餘 7.22 億元， YoY+28.76% ，預估稅後 EPS 13.00 元。  
五. 投資建議買進，目標價 208 元 
觀察裕慶 2026 年 ROE25% ，PER 12.3X ，ROE-PER 評價偏低，  
1Q~2Q26 營運呈現年增，有助評價提升，故投資建議買進，目標
價 208 元。 (16X2026 年稅後 EPS )

2 
貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
裕慶金屬成立於 1998 年，資本額 5.55 億元，並於 2024 年掛牌
上市。裕慶早期主要以展示架產品，提供全球大型零售通路商，
作為商品陳設。之後更跨足服飾、飲料、飯店、製鞋、油漆、傢
俱等全球領導品牌的商用展示架；此外，居家生活用的電動床，
其內部精密的金屬結構與製造，也得到客戶的認同，成為全球獨
家製造廠。更立基於此，近年投入資源，透過電子設備提供更有
價值的訊息，為居家生活提供更完整的加值服務。

圖一、裕慶公司沿革

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理

二. 投資架構與生產基地概況  
開曼裕慶旗下有五間子公司。台灣分公司位於台灣南投，主要以
營運總部，生產製造，南投廠占整體產能約 10~15% 。郁智康，
主要以電子裝置零組件開發為主，主要用於智能電動床機構件。
蘇州裕慶主要為生產製造為主，蘇州廠占整體產能約 85~90% 。
環宇木業，為裕慶 2017 年收購，主要以木質生產基地為主，搭配
裕慶原有金屬材質商用展示架。美國芝加哥子公司  Beeline 之設
計團隊與在地倉儲服務。

圖二、裕慶投資組織架構

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理

三. 2026 年營運概況及展望  
裕慶主要從事各類材質商用展示架與智能電動床機件之研發，生
產及銷售，展場設計服務，與居家健康管理智能模組研發等。

圖三、裕慶主要產品—商用展示架與智能電動床機構件

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理

預估商用展示架 2026 年營收金額 43 億元， YoY+24.64%

商用展示架產業具有高度競爭市場，其競爭情況受多個因素影
響，包括生產要素的價格波動、產品與消費需求波動、供應鏈變
化、創新和全球化等不同因素，環環相扣。裕慶生產各類材質的
商用展示架，從設計、工程結構以及製造多年經驗，客戶主要為
北美知名品牌或零售業者 ， 客戶包括 Adidas、 BOOT BARN 、Coca 
Cola、DICKS、HOME DEPOT 、H&M、KOHLS 、LOWES 、MUJI 、 
TARGET 、UNIQLO 與 Walmart 等。

4

商用展示架主要市場在美國，其中中國出口至美國關稅課徵高額
關稅，加上台幣升值影響，許多客戶訂單呈現減少情況，但其中
不乏逆勢成長客戶，包括： 1，美國家居裝修零售量販店在自動灑
水展示架出貨量持續增加，加上拖拉車訂單挹注，貢獻裕慶 2025
年營收金額 5.6 億元， YoY+102% 。2，美國跨國零售公司，在零
售賣場的展示架翻新計畫 ， 貢獻裕慶 2025 年營收金額 3.15 億元 ，
呈現倍數成長。 3，美國折扣零售百貨集團訂單己於 4Q24 小量出
貨， 2025 年開始放量， 2025 年貢獻裕慶營收金額 1.56 億元，呈
現倍增。商用展示架 2025 年營收金額 34.50 億元， YoY+4.2% 。

商用展示架 2026 年營運成長動能包括： 1，美國家居裝修零售量
販店在自動灑水展示架出貨量持續增加 ， 預估貢獻裕慶 2026 年營
收金額 8~8.5 億元， YoY+42~51% 。2，美國折扣零售百貨持續增
加原有門市汰舊換新訂單，另外也有新店展示架訂單，預期貢獻
2026 年營收金額 6~6.5 億元， YoY+130~170% 。3，美國跨國零
售公司，在零售賣場的展示架翻新計畫，新據點展示架，加上客
戶將商示架訂單集中至裕慶，預計 2026 年貢獻裕慶營收金額
4.5~5 億元， YoY+42~59% 。4，裕慶新業務方面，己取國內某居
家門鎖公司 60% 股權，初期 2026 年客戶將以大型零售通路與電
商通路為主，預估貢獻 2026 年營收金額 0.5~1 億元。綜合上述，
宏遠預估商用展示架 2026 年營收金額 43.00 億元 ，YoY+24.64%。

預估智能電動床機構件 2026 年營收金額 3 億元， YoY-19.8%

智能電動床主要消費市場在於歐美地區，智能電動床最初鎖定消
費者為行動不便的老年人口，消費族群絕大多數為 50 歲以上人
口，但隨著消費生活品質提升，消費結構的升級與業者的推廣，
越來愈多年輕族群開始購買智能電動床，根據國際睡眠品質協會
統計，智能電動床美國地區銷售量由 2016 年的 105.09 萬張成長
至 2022 年的 207.83 萬張，年複合成長率 12.04% 。隨著互聯網
普及與消費習慣提升後，智能電動床將逐漸吞食一般床的市場份
額，其市場仍具有成長空間。裕慶智能電動床機構件主要客戶為
美國 Leggett ＆Platt 。

裕慶 2025 年，智能電動床客戶在美國關稅因素，減少訂單，受到
主要客戶訂單減少影響 ， 預估智能電動床機構件 2025 年營收金額
3.74 億元， YoY-46.88%

預期 2026 年，在美國關稅成本增加，導致民間消費通膨，民眾將
消費將以必需品為主，非必要品將會被排擠，恐不利智能電動床
銷售 ， 預估智能電動床機構件 2026 年營收金額 3 億元 ，YoY-19.8%

華洋精機 (6983 TT)

展望樂觀

5/25 掛牌，展望樂觀 
1. 先進製程與封裝升級，推升高精度檢測需求 
2. 布局 TGV 檢測，掌握玻璃基板封裝成長機會 
3. SFO 光學技術建立差異化檢測門檻 
先進製程與封裝升級，推升高精度檢測需求 
受惠於先進製程微縮、AI/HPC 與 HBM 需求快速成長，晶圓製造與先進封裝對高
精度檢測設備的依賴度持續提升。華洋目前可提供 EUV/DUV 光罩、晶圓及先進封
裝製程檢測設備，應用涵蓋曝光、顯影、蝕刻、除膜、鍍膜、離子佈植、擴散與化
學拋光研磨等製程節點，主要針對光罩石英面、保護膜、外框 ，以及晶圓與CoWoS
先進封裝表面的微粒、髒污與外觀異常進行檢測，有助於即時辨識製程缺陷並提升
良率控管能力。隨 2.5D/3D 封裝、異質整合與多晶粒模組架構日益複雜，晶片對位
誤差 、 互連缺陷 、 材料裂紋 、 空隙 、 分層及熱膨脹係數不匹配所造成的翹曲與裂縫 ，
皆可能影響高價模組良率，使檢測與量測設備的重要性顯著提升。根據研調機構預
估，2025 年全球半導體晶圓光罩缺陷檢測設備市場規模為 20-25 億美元，並在未
來四年以 5%的 CAGR 持續成長。華洋預計今年完成晶圓線路檢測設備開發，進一
步切入晶圓製程尺寸量測應用。

布局 TGV 檢測，掌握玻璃基板封裝成長機會 
隨先進封裝技術由晶圓級封裝逐步延伸至面板級封裝，玻璃基板因具備大尺寸、高
平整度與優異電性特性，被視為下一階段高階封裝關鍵材料。應用型態可分為玻璃
載板、玻璃中介層與玻璃芯基板，其中現階段仍以玻璃載板為主；未來若技術進一
步推進至玻璃芯基板架構 ，TGV(玻璃穿孔)將成為核心製程之一 。華洋目前正發展涵
蓋雷射改質及 Via 蝕刻後穿孔檢測的完整 TGV 製程檢測方案。TGV 製程涵蓋玻璃
基板來料檢驗、通孔成形、金屬填孔與化學拋光研磨平坦化等多道步驟，各站點若
出現孔位偏移、孔形不一致、蝕刻異常或填孔缺陷，皆可能在後續製程中被放大，
影響封裝良率與可靠度，因此對高精度檢測與製程監控需求明顯提升。根據研調機
構預估，全球 TGV 玻璃基板市場規模預估將由 2025 年的 1.75 億美元，以 34.2%
的 CAGR 成長至 2033 年的 18 億美元。

SFO 光學技術建立差異化檢測門檻 
華洋擁有自主研發能力與獨家 SFO 表面光學技術 。 公司具備光學取像模組 、 影像處
理函式庫 、 瑕疵檢測技術與AI 演算法等核心能力 ， 可依客戶製程需求進行光學與光
源客製化設計，提升設備導入彈性與客戶黏著度。相較傳統Non-SFO 技術需先拍
攝原始影像 ， 再透過軟體濾除背景圖案後進行缺陷辨識 ， 華洋的SFO 技術可透過專
利光源設計，於光學端直接取得無底圖影像，降低背景干擾與演算法運算時間，並
提升缺陷辨識速度、尺寸量測準確性及檢測穩定度，有助於降低誤判與漏檢風險。
此技術尤其適用於光罩圖案區與更小尺寸微粒檢測，符合先進製程對高精度檢測的
要求。此外，檢測範圍不僅涵蓋微粒、纖維、刮傷等凸起型缺陷，亦可辨識酒精痕、
水痕、髒污、指紋及保護膜破裂等平面型瑕疵，檢測完整度優於多數僅聚焦單點或
單一缺陷型態的同業。華洋亦為台灣少數可同時應用於 EUV 與 DUV 光罩微粒檢測
的設備供應商。整體而言，華洋憑藉SFO 光學技術與自主開發能力，已建立高階檢
測設備的差異化門檻，有望受惠先進製程與先進封裝檢測需求成長，富邦對華洋後
續展望持正向看法。

評價和風險 
承銷價 85 元，上櫃前股價區間為 300-500 元，目前 Bloomberg 市場共識預估
2026/2027 年 EPS 為 5.80/7.11 元， 本益比區間交易在 FY27 的 42-70 倍，目前承
銷價居本益比區間下緣 ， 看法正向。 主要風險來自於先進製程與封裝需求不如預期 、
同業競爭加劇。

公司簡介

深耕半導體光學檢測 
華洋精機成立於 2012 年，專注於視覺影像檢測設備、自動化系統及客製化影像模
組的設計、開發、製造與銷售，主要提供半導體自動化光學檢測設備．公司核心產
品為光罩檢測機，涵蓋 EUV 及 DUV 光罩檢測，提供光罩圖案面、光罩玻璃面、保
護膜、保護膜框架進行檢查、識別缺陷，產品應用領域涵蓋半導體、印刷電路板、
平面顯示器及汽車產業 。2025 年營收 影像檢測設備占  71.25%，影像檢測模組  
16.3%、其他 11.65%。

Fubon's Rating System 
Rating Definition 
Buy Expected absolute return to be over 15% within the next 6 months  
Neutral  Expected absolute return to be level within the next 6 months  
Sell  Expected absolute return to be over negative 15% within the next 6 months 
Not Rated (NR)  Pursuant to Fubon acting in deals involving the company or there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an investment rating  
Under Review Fubon is in the process of determining an investment rating and will assign a rating within the 3- to 6-month horizon

Sector Rating Definition 
Overweight Sector expected to outperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months 
Market Weight  Sector expected to perform in line with the broader market averages within the next 6-12 months 
Underweight  Sector expected to underperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months.

個股報告 
新代(7750) 
轉單效應與市佔擴張雙軌並進 
投資評等 目標價 
逢低買進 $2960 
前次投資建議 2025.12.15： 
「逢低買進」 ，目標價: $1060

報告日期：2026.05.25 研究員：陳威仲

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 基於 26Q1 營收與獲利皆超預期，且看好中長期新代持續擴大中國市占率，與2027 年馬來
西亞新廠完工後，海外新興市場帶來之成長性，目標價由 1060 元(23 x 26(F)PER)調整至
2960 元(35 x 26(F)PER)，維持【逢低買進】投資評等。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/05/22 收盤價： 2,390.0 ➢ 26Q1 營收 48.7 億元，QoQ+31.7%/YoY+49.5%，大幅優
於兆豐預期 25.6%；毛利率 48.8%，季增 3.0/年增 4.2個
百分點，優於兆豐前次預期 4.2 個百分點，主因高階控制
器營收占比提升，與主要來自於同業缺料、漲價所帶來的
短期高毛利溢價與規模經濟；稅後淨利 10.7 億元，
YoY+123.0%，EPS 15.4 元，優於前次預期的 7.5元。 
➢ 受惠於高階自動化市場需求回溫，新代目前控制器主要競
爭對手(如西門子、三菱等)面臨需求激增之下，同時記憶
體與 IC 元件等控制器外購材料也面臨預期持續上漲之
下，使同業控制器宣布漲價之外， 交期也大幅拉長 (由
25Q4 的 1個月拉長至約 3個月)，迎來轉單效應。 
➢ 新代集團下的聯達智能 2025 年機械手臂銷量突破 1000
台，而 2026 年 1~5月，聯達手臂結合雲端方案的營收年
增率約為 40~50%，子公司事業事業同步成長。 
➢ 預估新代 2026 年稅後淨利為 60.8 億元，YoY+166.2%，
EPS84.6 元，目前股價位於 28 x26(F)PER，位於過去
25~36X 的下緣，基於 26Q1 營收與獲利皆超預期，且看
好中長期新代持續擴大中國市占率，與 2027 年馬來西亞
新廠完工後，海外新興市場帶來之成長性，目標價由1060
元(23 x 26(F)PER)調整至 2960 元(35 x 26(F)PER)，維
持【逢低買進】投資評等，潛在上漲空間 23.9%。

營 運 分 析

新代科技為亞太市場精密機械控制器領導廠商 
新代科技股份有限公司成立於 1995 年，專注於精密機械控制器領域，主要
銷售 CNC 控制器、伺服馬達，並積極開發工業型機械手臂、數控雲端軟體
與自動化設備解決方案等，持續提升產品競爭力與營運多角化。新代客戶多
為工具機與精密加工設備廠商，終端應用產業約為汽車(30%)、刀具模具與
通用機械(25%)、3C(15%)、其他(30%)，目前新代於中國控制器市場市佔率
已達約 25%，隨後則為發那科(FANUC)的 20%，新代近年已成為亞太市場
精密機械控制器領導廠商。

26Q1 自動化控制器需求激增帶動新代營收與獲利創高 
新代 26Q1 營收 48.7 億元，QoQ+31.7%/YoY+49.5%，大幅優於兆豐預期
25.6%，主因 25Q4 起中國自動化控制器需求激增，交期拉長與同業漲價之
下，轉單效應顯著；毛利率 48.8%，季增 3.0/年增 4.2 個百分點，優於兆豐
前次預期 4.2 個百分點，主因高階控制器營收占比提升，與主要來自於同業
缺料、漲價所帶來的短期高毛利溢價與規模經濟 ；稅後淨利 10.7 億元，
YoY+123.0%，EPS 15.4 元，優於前次預期的 7.5 元。

同業交期拉長創造轉單紅利，定價策略與成本控管穩固市占率 
受惠於高階自動化市場需求回溫，新代目前控制器主要競爭對手(如西門子、
三菱等)面臨需求激增之下，同時記憶體與 IC 元件等控制器外購材料也面臨
預期持續上漲之下，使同業控制器宣布漲價之外，交期也大幅拉長(由 25Q4
的 1個月拉長至約 3個月)。而中國正值工業設備汰舊換新之週期，若下游工
業客戶不趁現在汰換或購置控制器，在預期原料持續上漲且產業交期持續拉
長之下，若遇到控制器的無貨可發，將面對產線的停機風險與大幅的損失。
而新代相較於同業選擇漲價，初期仍選擇穩定報價，除了創造目前高階控制
器的持續轉單，也利於在中國市占率的持續擴張。 
而針對市場擔憂的 DRAM 記憶體漲價風險，目前主要的挑戰在於料件的取
得難度，新代已採取多供應商策略以確保料源無虞，預期記憶體成本上升的
遞延效應將落在 26Q2~26Q3 浮現，但由於軟體與主板設計為新代 100%自
製，外部零組件成本的衝擊在可控範圍內，即便未將成本完全轉嫁給客戶，
仍預期能維持公司整體 45%的長期目標毛利率。 
兆豐預估新代在交期速度與 ASP競爭力皆優於同業，在成本可控之下，高階
控制器產品將持續受惠轉單效益。目前全球 AI製造鏈原料成本皆持續上漲之
下，預估自動化設備需求端與成本端推動的螺旋上漲將持續，且印度與東南
亞等新興市場營收增速持續優於公司整體水準，隨著 2027 年馬來西亞新廠
啟用，預估將帶動新代 2026~2027 年控制器出貨套數年增率維持在高雙位數
水準。

百萬元

子公司聯達智能與正鉑雷射隨新代控制器的市占上升同步成長 
隨著製造業終端朝向智慧彈性製造轉型，新代集團下的聯達智能透過深度整
合 CNC 與機械手臂的策略，2025 年機械手臂銷量突破 1000 台，而 2026
年 1~5月，聯達手臂結合雲端方案的營收年增率約為 40~50%，顯示從單機
智慧走向單元自動化的策略正快速變現。且新代與聯達於 4/27日正式與中國
華沿機器人簽署戰略合作備忘錄，確立了在三年內共同推動 10,000 台協作
機器人的銷售目 標。而子公司正鉑雷射正積極將 既有的工廠自動化統包方
案，向上延伸至高附加價值的半導體設備領域。其中除了再生晶圓雷射除膜
技術之外，針對特殊材料(如玻璃)進行表面標記與改質的技術能量，結合其
在切割與精微加工的深厚基礎，正鉑針對玻璃基板所開發的雷射鑽孔與微加
工設備，有望順應未來先進封裝與高階載板材料升級的產業趨勢。

- 5 - 
個股報告 
預估新代中國市占率持續上升，且海外市場與子公司表現同步轉強 
兆豐預估 2026 年新代將持續因中國市占率的提升與轉單效益，而使控制器
事業持續維持高雙位數增長 ，且海外市場與子公司仍在基期較低的 爆發階
段，預估新代 2026 年營收為 264.2 億元，YoY+89.2%；惟預估 26Q1 較高
毛利為短期的高階控制器營收占比急升所致，而隨著蘇州二廠於 26Q2 完工
啟用後，且記憶體與 IC 元件的持續上漲，將對毛利產生部分壓力， 預估
26Q2~26H2 將逐步反應成本的上升，使毛利率回到 45~47%的區間。 
預估新代 2026 年稅後淨利為 60.8 億元，YoY+166.2%，EPS84.6 元，目前
股價位於 28 x26(F)PER，位於過去 25~36X的下緣，基於 26Q1 營收與獲利
皆超預期，且看好中長期新代持續擴大中國市占率，與 2027 年馬來西亞新
廠完工後，海外新興市場帶來之成長性，目標價由 1060 元(23 x 26(F)PER)
調整至 2960元(35 x 26(F)PER)，維持【逢低買進】投資評等，潛在上漲空
間 23.9%。

公司簡介 
翔名為半導體設備零組件代工廠 ， 成立初期
以代理日商幫浦產品 、 石墨承載盤為主要業
務，2023 年併購芝和精密，切入前段蝕刻
設備所用電極板 、 矽環等 ， 目前主要透過精
密加工、表面處理、雷熱熔覆、電子束焊接
等技術生產如石墨及金屬件 、 合金件等半導
體設備零組件的製造翻修 ， 現為台灣半導體
用離子植入機最大代工廠。

主要客戶： 美商應用材料 (AMAT US) 、
ASML、晶圓製造廠等 
主要競爭對手：

王彥鈞 stanley.wang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：座談會

翔名 (8091 TT) 
產業成長、業務拓展

永豐觀點 
1Q26 營運優於預期，主因成本轉嫁和低價庫存帶動獲利提升。研究部預期翔名受
惠半導體產業上升週期，對設備、耗材需求，在 2026-2027 年營運持續創高。 
 投資評價與建議 
目標價升至 271 元：翔名 1Q26 營運創高且 2-3Q26 有望續揚，短期受惠半導體
及航太專案、適度轉嫁原料成本而成長，中期亦受惠半導體製造廠產能利用率提
升、全球擴產，帶動設備及耗材的直接/間接需求。研究部維持翔名買進建議，目
標價則升至 271 元(25x 2027 EPSF)。

### Page 2

2

翔名為半導體設備零組件代工廠：翔名成立於 1991 年，2004 年上櫃，資本額約
5.27 億，成立初期以代理日商幫浦產品、石墨承載盤為主要業務，2023 年併購芝
和精密，切入前段蝕刻設備所用電極板、矽環等，目前主要透過精密加工、表面處
理、雷熱熔覆、電子束焊接等技術生產如石墨及金屬件、合金件等半導體設備零組
件的製造翻修，現為台灣半導體用離子植入機最大代工廠。總部位於新竹香山，生
產據點則位在新竹、南京，並在日本熊本、嘉義馬稠後有擴廠計畫。主要客戶為美
商應用材料(AMAT US)、ASML、晶圓製造廠等。 
1Q26 營收、獲利雙雙創歷史紀錄： 翔名 1Q26 營收 6.5 億(+16.9%QoQ，
+38.0%YoY)， 其中半導體相關業務成長8%QoQ、 光電相關業務成長286%QoQ，
分別受惠於(1) 特化管路通過驗證，於 1Q26 開始放量，(2) 開始將貴金屬漲價成
本反映給客戶，和(3) 專案出貨認列，毛利率達 40.6%的新高水準，係因較好的產
品組 合 、匯 率 、即 啟動 漲 價以 反 映成 本 ，稅 後淨利 1.3 億(+33.1%QoQ，
+107.8%YoY)，EPS 2.56 元，大幅優於研究部預期的 1.4 元。 
隨產業、新專案放量：研究部預期翔名 2026 年將隨(1) 晶圓代工廠產能利用率回
升(先進製程滿載，成熟製程陸續回到 80%以上)，帶動耗材需求，(2) 設備原廠客
戶需求提升 ，主要客戶先前在法說預期全年半導體設備銷售年增30%YoY， 且將與
供應鏈共同擴張產能，(3) 2024-2025 年所接的航太訂單於 2026 年陸續認列，(4) 
鎢 、 鉬等貴金屬成本在2025 年持續墊高 ， 翔名自2026 年起陸續反映成本給客戶 ，
預期 3Q26 將進行第二波價格調整 ，(4) 新專案如特化管路用於先進製程化學品供
應系統、異質焊接、陶瓷度膜、光學反射器用於爐管設備中等，預期2026 年整體
新專案營收貢獻將翻倍。 
廠務調整中： (1) 熊本廠已於 4/15 開幕，目前客戶正在驗證產線，後續有望在
4Q26-1Q27 起貢獻營收，(2) 嘉義廠維持 2027 年底完工之預期，因應蝕刻設備
客戶、子公司等需求，自2028 年起投產。 (3) 南京廠持續增加投入，以滿足在地
化客戶對半導體耗材需求，2026 年可維持雙位數成長。 
財務預估：研究部預估翔名 2026 年營收 28.6 億(+33.8%YoY)，毛利率 37.8%，
稅後淨利 4.9 億(+61.4%YoY)，EPS 9.32 元(原估 8.47 元)。2027 年營收 32.5 億
(+13.9%YoY)，毛利率 39.3%，稅後淨利 5.8 億(+17.8%YoY)，EPS 10.86 元。

### Page 1

growth for the AI industry, with API token growth of +60% Q/Q
in 1Q and daily tokens generated up to 23T . We view these
metrics as a decent proxy for leading lab usage, in addition to
the ARR callouts from Anthropic and OpenAI.
The following companies are privately held and are not under coverage by Barclays Research:
OpenAI and Anthropic. Information about these companies is being provided for information
purposes only and is not an investment recommendation by Barclays Research.

The Key Take-Away: API token growth is surging across the AI industry, a result of agentic
enterprise products finally reaching the deployment stage. For most of last year, AI adoption
was narrowly focused on deterministic fields like code writing and customer service, but agents
like Anthropic Claude Code & Cowork and OpenAI Codex are starting to move the diffusion out
to other segments of the economy. Google recently disclosed the latest on API tokens generated

fears around infrastructure (AI capex) and recent articles pointing to OpenAI missing 2025
revenue targets. As the level of transparency in AI KPIs increases, especially as IPOs enter the
discussion later this year, we expect the "long AI" trade to continue to work.
The Charts & Data: Below we show various charts on data from Google Cloud Next '26, GitHub
activity, leading AI lab API run rates, and our updated hyperscaler capex estimates.
"Our first-party models now process more than 16 billion tokens per minute via direct API use

by our customers, up from 10 billion last quarter. " - Google Cloud Next '26
Google's direct Gemini API has seen rapid growth on the back of new model releases over the
past few months, including the Gemini 3.1 Pro and 3.1 Flash-Lite models. The Gemini models
offer particularly good price performance, and the recent takeoff in coding agents and app
builders is likely contributing to this inflection in tokens processed. In Figure 1, we show how
Gemini API token usage has trended over the past 6 months.

BCI, US
Completed: 30-Apr-26, 22:04 GMT Released: 01-May-26, 04:10 GMT Restricted - External

### Page 2

"Since the second half of December 2025, agentic development workflows have accelerated
sharply. By nearly every measure, the direction is already clear: repository creation, pull
request activity, API usage, automation, and large-repository workloads are all growing
quickly. " - GitHub
As the largest platform for hosting/collaborating on source code, GitHub has a direct view into
how much AI has diffused into software development. A recent activity update showed
exponential growth in both commits (changes to code; Figure 2) and new repositories
(uploaded projects; Figure 3). Similar to Gemini's increased API usage, this rapid change at
GitHub shows that agentic development workflows have accelerated code creation in recent
months, and we expect that trend to make its way into other areas of white-collar work shortly.

"In fact, nearly 75% of Google Cloud customers are using our AI products to power their
businesses. In addition, over the past 12 months, 330 Google Cloud customers each processed
more than one trillion tokens, while 35 reached the 10-trillion-token milestone with our
models. " - Google Cloud Next '26

off stage. Anthropic likely has gotten some extra growth on the back of the Opus 4.6/4.7 releases
and Claude Code adoption and the generally strong sentiment towards their enterprise
products in '26 (based on Anthropic's funding announcement in February, we'd guess the
company is seeing Claude Code and Cowork show the fastest revenue bump of any segment
right now).

Guide to the Barclays Fundamental Equity Research Rating System:
Our coverage analysts use a relative rating system in which they rate stocks as Overweight, Equal Weight or Underweight (see definitions below)
relative to other companies covered by the analyst or a team of analysts that are deemed to be in the same industry (the "industry coverage universe").

In addition to the stock rating, we provide industry views which rate the outlook for the industry coverage universe as Positive, Neutral or Negative (see
definitions below). A rating system using terms such as buy, hold and sell is not the equivalent of our rating system. Investors should carefully read the
entire research report including the definitions of all ratings and not infer its contents from ratings alone.
Stock Rating
Overweight - The stock is expected to outperform the unweighted expected total return of the industry coverage universe over a 12-month investment

horizon.
Equal Weight - The stock is expected to perform in line with the unweighted expected total return of the industry coverage universe over a 12-month
investment horizon.
Underweight - The stock is expected to underperform the unweighted expected total return of the industry coverage universe over a 12-month
investment horizon.
Rating Suspended - The rating and target price have been suspended temporarily due to market events that made coverage impracticable or to

comply with applicable regulations and/or firm policies in certain circumstances including where the Investment Bank of Barclays Bank PLC is acting in
an advisory capacity in a merger or strategic transaction involving the company.
Industry View
Positive - industry coverage universe fundamentals/valuations are improving.
Neutral - industry coverage universe fundamentals/valuations are steady, neither improving nor deteriorating.
Negative - industry coverage universe fundamentals/valuations are deteriorating.

Valuation Methodology: We value shares of AMZN using a sum-of-the-parts methodology for its retail and AWS businesses. Our valuation of AMZN's

retail business is based on a 20x EBITDA multiple using a blend of our FY27/28 estimates; while we value AWS using a 6x multiple on a blend of our
FY27/28 revenue estimates. Our resulting price target is $330.
Risks which May Impede the Achievement of the Barclays Research Valuation and Price Target: Downside risks in the short term that could
negatively impact our model and consensus estimates include 1) greater-than-expected slowdown in AWS against the weak macro backdrop, and

hence lower-than-expected OI margins, over a more drawn-out period; 2) further erosion in consumer spending and a persistent input/wage cost
inflation could pressure the Retail business. Longer-term risks include the highly competitive ecommerce landscape, increasing shipping costs from
same and next-day delivery, which could be a drag on margins, security breaches or frequent downtime in the AWS business, which could slow down
public cloud adoption, or execution missteps that could slow growth of the retail business.

• After an extended bearish stance, where EM have been on a relative downtrend
ever since 2008, we turned OW EM equities last year, and argued in our ’26

Year Ahead that this should be their second consecutive year of outperformance.
We refresh arguments over why EM are likely to have a further meaningful
rally in 2H of the year:
• 1. AI trade continuing . In March we highlighted that Mag-7 moved to the
cheapest in 10 years, and AI at risk stocks became record cheap. The attractive
valuations, underpinned by strong earnings, helped the space rally. EM AI plays
are in our view even cheaper and offer further upside potential, and represent a

big chunk of the overall index, at 40% of MSCI EM. Our EM strategists stay
bullish on Korea and Taiwan.  Here, memory rally has been aggressive, but
meaningful supply additions are not arriving before 2H of next year, with
equities typically discounting the worsening supply-demand balance 6-9
months ahead, so it would be too early to turn bearish already. We also believe
that China Tech should catch up, as well, and it is trading at near record discounts

to others.
• 2. Fed, bond yields and USD view. We continue to believe that market pricing
of central banks’ hawkishness, which has spiraled since the Iran war started, is
likely to reverse in 2H - differences to 2022 remain dramatic , in terms of
corporate pricing power, wage growth, real rates, etc. - see our report. With a new
Fed chairman arriving, the wildcard is that some of the Fed’s reaction function
shifts more dovish later in the year. Bond yields could drift lower in 2H, either

way the conflict proceeds from here. USD has acted as a safe haven in the past
2 months, but we note that it is trading expensive in a long-term context, to the
tune of 10-15%. If USD is softer, EM tended to benefit.
• 3. China green shoots? In our Year Ahead, we argued that China activity was
likely to inflect higher, given the liquidity infusion, supportive policy stance and
a stabilization in house prices. We are seeing increasing evidence of this. The

upcoming Trump-Xi meeting could aid the outlook.
• 4. Iran conflict to subside. We have consistently argued since the second half
of March to use the dips driven by geopolitical uncertainty to buy into. We
continue to see bouts of weakness as opportunities to add, and don’t find equities
as complacent, despite fresh all-time highs for both MXWO and for MXEF. If
the geopolitical situation stabilizes in 2H, with easing concerns over oil supplies,
EM stand to benefit.

• 5. Valuations, positioning and flows are all attractive for EM. EM P/E relative
to DM is trading at record cheap at present - middle chart, and at absolute 12x
forward P/E the space is far from demanding. Investor positioning remains low
in EM, and flows are accelerating - bottom chart.
• We stay very bullish on Mining, Semis and Industrials  sectors. Cyclical
Consumer plays are trading at relative lows, which likely stays the case until the
Iran conflict shows more concrete evidence of waning, but are likely to have a

After an extended bearish stance on Emerging markets, where
EM equities were on a relative downtrend ever since 2018,
we turned Overweight EM equities last year and argued in

our 2026 Year Ahead that this should be their second consec-
utive year of outperformance. Since the beginning of 2025,
EM equities have cumulatively outperformed DM by approx-
imately 25% in dollar terms. However, this gain remains
modest compared to the 47% relative underperformance that
EM experienced versus DM from 2018 to 2024, suggesting
the reversal has considerably further to run.

MSCI EM relative to DM ($)

The multi-year underperformance of EM versus DM from
2018 to 2024 was one of the longest and deepest relative
drawdowns on record. This was mainly driven by a strong
dollar, China's structural slowdown, and the dominance of US
mega-cap technology sector. We believe the conditions that
sustained this underperformance are now reversing, the dollar

is expensive and likely to weaken, China policy is turning
supportive, and EM AI plays offer a valuation-attractive alter-
native to crowded US tech positions. The 2025 outperfor-
mance of 9% and the 2026 YTD gain of approximately 15%
represent, in our view, only the early stages of a multi-year
mean reversion.

Despite the disruption caused by the Iran conflict in Q1, EM
equities have demonstrated resilience, with MSCI EM mak-
ing fresh all-time highs alongside MSCI World. We do not
find equities complacent at these levels, given the supportive
fundamental backdrop and the degree of underinvestment that
persists in the asset class.
In this report, we refresh our arguments over why EM is like-
ly to have a further meaningful rally in the second half of the

year.
1)  EM offers cheaper exposure to the resurgent AI trade

One of the key concerns around the AI trade earlier was the
difficulty in monetisation. The worry was that, with no clear
moat in AI services, value creation would accrue predomi-

nantly to consumers rather than providers, eventually leading
to a reassessment of spending plans. Recent developments
have materially shifted this debate. Anthropic's disclosed rev-
enue acceleration, from approximately $9bn ARR at the end
of 2025 to approximately $30bn ARR in April 2026, has reig-
nited the bullish AI trade. Token consumption growth has
accelerated from approximately 10x year-on-year at end 2025
to approximately 20x currently, and we are seeing a modera-

tion of pricing deflation with various approaches to throttle
token usage and increase pricing with new model launches.
Figure 6: High Bandwidth Memory Revenue (HBM)

HBM Revenue ($bn)
Source: J.P. Morgan. Korea Technology team
Beyond the improving monetization picture, tightness in the
AI compute supply chain is filtering through to pricing
increases across the board. The memory rally has been

aggressive, with DRAM prices more than tripling from their
2023 trough. Samsung Electronics posted an eightfold
increase in quarterly profit, with chips responsible for 94% of
its record total, while SK Hynix reported a fivefold increase
in quarterly earnings. Together, these two companies account
for almost 55% of MSCI Korea.
However, meaningful supply additions are not arriving before
the second half of 2027. New fab capacity from Samsung and
SK Hynix is not expected to come online until late next year

at the earliest, and the conversion of existing DRAM lines to
HBM further constrains conventional memory supply. Equi-
ties typically discount a worsening supply demand balance 6
to 9 months ahead of the physical market turning, which sug-
gests it would be too early to turn bearish on memory names
already. Better risk management, in our view, may be in being
slightly late rather than too early in positioning for a cycle
downturn, an approach we have advocated for in Korea over

the past six months.
This is a meaningful positive for the entire AI hardware sup-
ply chain, which is overwhelmingly domiciled in EM Asia.

US tech giants are expected to spend close to $700 billion in
capex this year, much of it directed toward AI infrastructure.

Nvidia now relies on Asian suppliers for approximately 90%
of its production costs, up from 65% last year. The tech capex
outlook is likely to be revised even higher on the back of
recent cybersecurity-risk-related headlines, as the private and
public sectors actively look to minimize system vulnerabili-
ties and modernise legacy infrastructure.

We continue to believe that market pricing of central bank
hawkishness, which spiralled since the Iran conflict started, is
likely to reverse in the second half of the year. Markets
moved ECB rate forecasts up by as much as 70bp since the
initial escalation, and taken out expectations for Fed cuts by
almost 60bp. Pricing of end-2026 Fed funds futures remains
well above pre-conflict levels. We believe this hawkish
repricing is set to unwind.

While an extended conflict could push prices higher in the
near term, the differences to 2022 remain dramatic. Wage
growth is decelerating rather than accelerating, corporate

pricing power is fading, real rates are already restrictive, and
the oil intensity of GDP is roughly 60% lower than in the
1970s. Central banks entered 2022 with policy rates well
below neutral and were forced to catch up aggressively. At
present, policy rates are largely in line with historical norms.
With a new Fed chairman arriving later this year, the wild
card is that some of the Fed's reaction function shifts more
dovish. Bond yields could drift lower in the second half

regardless of how the conflict proceeds from here, as the
growth impulse from the oil shock fades and the disinflation-
ary forces in services and wages reassert themselves.

Bond yields across DM have remained elevated, 40-50bp
higher versus pre-conflict levels, altogether tightening finan-

cial conditions. We believe this represents an overshoot that
will correct as the conflict de-escalates and inflation expecta-
tions normalise. For EM assets, a decline in DM yields would
ease funding costs, support capital flows, and reduce the
opportunity cost of holding EM equities.

The dollar has acted as a safe haven in the past two months,

but we note that it is trading expensive in a long-term context,
to the tune of 10-15% above fair value on our FX team's
REER models. Our FX strategists' longer-term bearish dollar
view remains intact. They believe the dollar outlook is less
bearish than it was pre-conflict due to persistent energy pres-
sures, but it is not sufficient to alter the medium-term bearish
stance.

While consumption indicators remain subdued, we note that

increases, birth incentives, and childcare support. The 15th

for consumption to rise to approximately 700 billion yuan
(0.5% of GDP) in 2026.

related to Iran

Source: Datastream
We have consistently argued from the second half of March
that we should use the dips driven by geopolitical uncertainty
to buy into. The V-shaped rebound that materialised validated

this approach, and we continue to see any weakness as oppor-
tunities to add. We do not find equities complacent despite
fresh all-time highs for both MSCI World and MSCI EM.
The path to a negotiated settlement looks more visible now,
given constraints on both sides. While the situation remains
unresolved and poses non-linear risks to commodity supply
should it persist, the range of potential outcomes has substan-
tially diminished. Energy prices have moderated from their

peaks but remain well above pre-conflict levels. However, we
see very limited risk of the current situation devolving into a
2022 style energy crisis.

Should conditions improve, EM stands to benefit dispropor-

India, Korea, and Taiwan, would see a direct uplift to their
terms of trade and corporate margins, amplifying the positive
impact relative to developed market peers.

Equity Strategy Key Calls and Drivers
Iran conflict might need to escalate in order to de-escalate, but an oil spike and resultant market weakness might not sustain, as an escalation might in fact make an off-ramp more likely. The
considerations of swiftly rising US gasoline price and adverse polling on one side, and on the other the increasing discomfort among Iran’s largest oil customers become more acute as time passes. While

near term volatility is probable, we think that if one has a time horizon longer than next days/weeks, on 3/6/12 months timeframe one should continue using dips to add into. Now, is it obvious that there is
a disconnect between equities and commodity markets given that MXWO completed a V-shaped rebound, but at the same time Strait of Hormuz is staying closed? Not necessarily. Of course, if Brent
shows a non-linear move higher, equities will be lower. But also, similar to what transpired around Liberation day, the market can get whipsawed on any positive headline. Equities are not all that

complacent beneath the surface - current market breath is very narrow and nearly all consumer plays are lingering at lows. We see big differences to 2022 template, and do not think that stagflation is the
most likely outcome for 2H, while market earnings are staying on an uptrend, and we indicated in March that Mag-7 valuations turned outright cheap again. We looked for China green shoots - a pickup in
domestic activity, which should help our EM OW.

Explanation of Equity Research Ratings, Designations and Analyst(s) Coverage Universe:
J.P. Morgan uses the following rating system: Overweight (over the duration of the price target indicated in this report, we expect this stock will
outperform the average total return of the stocks in the Research Analyst’s, or the Research Analyst’s team’s, coverage universe); Neutral (over
the duration of the price target indicated in this report, we expect this stock will perform in line with the average total return of the stocks in the

Research Analyst’s, or the Research Analyst’s team’s, coverage universe); and Underweight (over the duration of the price target indicated in
this report, we expect this stock will underperform the average total return of the stocks in the Research Analyst’s, or the Research Analyst’s
team’s, coverage universe. NR is Not Rated. In this case, J.P. Morgan has removed the rating and, if applicable, the price target, for this stock
because of either a lack of a sufficient fundamental basis or for legal, regulatory or policy reasons. The previous rating and, if applicable, the

price target, no longer should be relied upon. An NR designation is not a recommendation or a rating. Some stocks under coverage have a rating
but no price target; in these cases, we expect the stock will outperform/perform in line/underperform the average total return of the stocks in the
Research Analyst’s, or the Research Analyst’s team’s, coverage universe of the relevant duration of the region. In our Asia (ex-Australia and ex-
India) and U.K. small- and mid-cap Equity Research, each stock’s expected total return is compared to the expected total return of a benchmark

the certifying Research Analyst’s coverage universe can be found on J.P. Morgan’s Research website, https://www.jpmorganmarkets.com.

Source:                         Trendforce, Morgan Stanley Research
We expect TV panel prices to start trending down in 3Q26 with
IT panels starting later (4Q26).  Fundamentals for core display
business should soften soon and the contribution from emerging

new offerings looks limited in the near term. EW on TW panel
names, with a relative preference for BOE.
TV panel prices flat MoM in May: Mainstream 32", 43", 55", 65" and 75" TV panel
pricing were all flat MoM for the month of May. This is consistent with our view. On
the demand side, TV panel shipments started to slow down as the early restocking
is tapering off. On the supply side, major panel makers have responded by lowering
utilization rates during the May holiday in China to align output with orders. We

believe that  TV panel shipments could be below seasonal, given the strong 1H26,
which would further weaken the bargaining power of panel makers. Thus, our base-
case assumption remains that TV panel prices should trend down from 3Q26, but
the extent should not be significant, since the supply-side discipline will protect on
the downside. On a quarterly average basis, we expect TV panel prices to increase
by low single-digits % QoQ in 2Q26.
IT panel prices  largely stable in May: Monitor panel prices edged up 0.4% MoM,

while  NBs were flat MoM. Demand for monitor panels is expected to hold up
relatively well, more consistent with seasonality, so panel makers still have the
upper hand in price negotiations in May. NB panel makers are less optimistic about
the demand outlook in 2H26 amid rising costs and tight supply of key components,
and so have been less aggressive in asking for price hikes in 2Q26. In general, we
expect IT panel prices to be relatively stable vs. TV panels in the coming quarters.

Nonetheless, the ramp up of the Gen 8.6 OLED fabs from Korea and China panel
makers should eat into the addressable market for LCD based IT panels, especially
NBs and tablets, thus adding more structural pricing pressure in these areas.
Risk/reward less attractive: With this round of price hike cycle  peaking, we think
valuations are less attractive at this stage, especially for Taiwan names AUO
(2409.TW; 0.9x P/B) and Innolux (3481.TW; 1.3x P/B). We understand that there

could be upside in the emerging businesses, where glass can be used in advanced
packaging architectures. We believe panel makers are not the only players
competing for those opportunities, and even if things move smoothly, more
meaningful contribution will take time. We therefore believe investing in panel
stocks from this angle might look too speculative now, since valuations are already
at or above  peak levels of the past few years. Within the A-share universe, we prefer

BOE (000725.SZ, 1.1x P/B) over TCL (000100.SZ, 1.5x P/B) on scale advantage,
higher concentration in the display business and cheaper valuation.

TV Panel Prices Seen Stabilizing in May/June
TV panel prices started to edge up at the beginning of this year, rising 1% MoM in January,
2% MoM in February and 2% MoM in March.  April was largely flat,  and we see prices
stabilizing in May/June before order momentum potentially tapers from late 2Q26.

Panel Shipments To Be Skewed Toward 1H26

Over the past decade, TV panel shipments have averaged a 49%/51% split between 1H/2H.
In 2026, we think the mix will skew toward 1H26 because TV brands restocked ahead of
major promotional events, including the Winter Olympics in February, the FIFA World Cup
in June-July, and the China 618 campaign. Although there is no clear evidence that these
events will significantly boost global TV demand, we have consistently seen shipment
timing shift between quarters around such promotions.

Display Fab Utilization to Stay at 80-85% in 2Q26
Supported by stronger TV panel shipments, display fab utilization averaged ~80% in 4Q24
and 82% in 1Q25. It fell to 78% in 2Q25 as brands turned more conservative after earlier
pull‑ins tapered and as the effect of China’s trade‑in program faded. Utilization rose to
80-85% in 3Q25 as restocking resumed for year‑end promotions, and stayed at a similar
level in 4Q25 despite seasonal softness as well as into 1Q26. We  think industry utilization

can remain roughly flat QoQ, at 80-85% in 2Q26 as output‑discipline measures stay in
place.
In the medium to long term, we maintain our view that the display segment is undergoing
structural change. Chinese panel makers have shifted strategically from top‑line growth
to profitability, with fewer government subsidies reinforcing supply discipline. We think
more investors now believe the industry’s “production to order” model can be sustained,
especially as panel price cycles shortened to quarters in 2023-24 vs. multiyear cycles in

the prior decade. Consolidation moves – including LG Display’s sale of its Gen 8.5
Guangzhou fab to TCL and Sharp’s shutdown of its Gen 10 Sakai fab – also support a
healthier supply backdrop (for more information, see Display – Structural Changes on
Supply Side Not Fairly Priced In,  June 17, 2021).

### Page 5

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 5
Risk/Reward Stays Less Attractive
We believe this round of panel price hikes is coming to an end, and thus risk/reward is less
attractive, especially for the Taiwanese names. Below are our current views:
• AUO (2409.TW, Derrick Yang): We are EW on AUO,  with a PT of NT$14.00, based
on a  0.7x 2026e P/B multiple. Although the current TV panel price hikes are
ongoing, we think valuation of 0.9x 2026e P/B has likely  priced in most of the

favorable dynamics vs. its mid-cycle average of 0.8x since 2022.
• BOE (000725.SZ, Derrick Yang): We have a relative preference for BOE, owing to
its scale advantage, more diversified revenue mix across applications and
technologies, and relatively cheap valuation vs. its Chinese peer TCL. Our PT of
Rmb5.20 is based on 1.4x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.2x since 2022,
and we think the premium is justified by its solid industry position.

• Innolux (3481.TW, Derrick Yang): We are EW on Innolux, with a price target of NT
$19.50, based on a  target multiple of 0.7x 2026e P/B (vs. mid-cycle average of 0.5x
since 2022). We think the valuation  premium implied by our target multiple over
its mid-cycle average  can be justified by the consolidation of Pioneer from
December 2025, which will likely bring its commodity revenue down to 45-50% in
2026e vs. 66% in 2024. For the FOPLP business, we think it could leverage its

existing technology expertise and legacy capacity, but more meaningful
contributions are likely to take time.
• LG Display (034220.KS, Shawn Kim): Meaningful OLED revenue contribution
should increasingly improve earnings from 2H25, but potentially weaker end
markets keep us from turning bullish on LGD. The stock trades at ~0.9x 2026e P/B
vs. a historical mid-cycle level of 0.5x.
• TCL (000100.SZ, Derrick Yang): We are EW with a price target of Rmb4.70 (1.7x

2026e P/B).  The stock is trading at ~1.5x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.4x
since 2022, so we think the panel price hike cycle has been largely baked into its
share price.
• Corning (GLW.N, Meta Marshall): GLW's display business operates efficiently and
sees further margin support  from additional currency-related price increases, while
the optical business resumes growth as carrier fiber deployments pick up. GLW's
display business closely correlates with panel maker utilization, and is its largest

earnings contributor.
• Greater China DDIC supply chain implications – Novatek (3034.TW, Daniel Yen):
Daniel's UW thesis is based on  weaker PC, smartphone and auto demand, gross
margin pressure and lack of new growth drivers. That said, Novatek's OLED DDI
roadmap looks more competitive than that of its key Korean competitors on
performance and cost, helping  it increase its market share in Apple's iPhone supply
chain.

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
Where Could We Be Wrong?
• Stronger end-demand: If end-demand for major applications turns out to be better
than expected, there could be  stronger panel shipments and less panel price
pressure.
• More stringent  output control: If major panel makers collectively adjust
utilization to an extreme extent, they could mitigate pricing pressure in the case of
softer demand.
• Non-commodity display business: If the non-commodity business progresses

faster than expected, panel makers could show better financial results.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over
the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.

Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 1

ab 21 May 2026
Global Research
China Equity Strategy
How crowded is the AI trade and other
frequently asked investor questions
Buoyant mood supported by earnings and improving fundamentals
In this note, we provide answers to five of the topical questions by investors in our recent
conversations. In general sentiment is buoyant with investors largely putting behind the
impact of Strait of Hormuz shutdown, anchored by solid earnings from hardware tech

companies. AI hardware tech seems to be the consensus long among investors with
PM's and generalists dedicating more time looking for alpha opportunities along the
supply chain though  some investors question the sustainability of the rally given
crowded positions. The property market recovery also garnered significant level of
interest from investors and while conviction level of a sustained recovery is not high, the
positive development is supportive for overall market sentiment. Another topic of

interest was whether the recent rise in PPI, driven by imported inflation, could weigh on
corporate earnings this time around. In addition, investors showed interest in the
potential re-rating of HSTECH though in general sentiment is poor around the internet
sector.
How crowded is the AI trade and what could derail the rally?
Current investor positioning of AI hardware names  is elevated but this has been the case
most of this year as evidenced by: 1) onshore mutual fund positioning in telecom and

electronics have been broadly stable in the last 3 quarters with a decline in electronics
allocation offset by higher allocation towards telecoms (Figure 2); 2) the average
crowding factor of a basket of popular AI names indicate that investor positioning has
risen this year, although there has been a modest pullback from the high recorded in
early May 2026 (Figure 3); 3) Turnover in the broader TMT sector is  elevated at ~37% of
total A-share turnover, compared with a historical peak of 43% in 2023. Looking at

regional flows and positions, foreign investor outflows from Taiwan and Korea equities
(both global AI proxies) this year  suggest some broad profit taking in this space.
Meanwhile, domestic token pricing has been rising  and while valuation is high with
semi's for example trading on 59x forward P/E (2.5 std. dev above historical average
shown in Figure 41), earnings growth in the sector has also been accelerating (up 64%
in 1Q26).  It is difficult to specifically pinpoint the events that could derail the tech rally

with some investors pointing to the rising US interest rates, IPO's of the large LLM
companies and a slowdown in earnings growth as potential sign-posts that could trigger
a re-assessment of positioning.
Would higher PPI lead to lower earnings?
Investors were concerned whether imported inflation would squeeze corporate margins
given the recent surge in energy prices. We think a higher PPI may not hurt overall listed
company earnings given the following observations: 1) higher PPI's historically have led

to higher revenue and earnings growth in listed companies. This relationship also held
up during the Russia–Ukraine war in 2022, when supply disruptions also drove a surge in
energy and transport prices; 2) exporters' earnings historically have done well in periods
of imported inflation as this gives companies an excuse to raise prices (Figure 23), 3)
listed companies generally have better ability to pass on the higher costs to customers -
historically utilities and food and beverage companies' earnings were shown to be the
